pub type skene_terminal::LayoutBox<Kind> = skene_foundation::layout_box::LayoutBox<skene_terminal::ActivePlatform, Kind>
pub type skene_terminal::Media = skene_foundation::spine::condition::Media<u32>
pub type skene_terminal::NodeHandle = skene_foundation::node_handle::NodeHandle<skene_terminal::ActivePlatform>
pub type skene_terminal::Point = skene_foundation::spine::values::point::Point<u32>
pub type skene_terminal::Rect = skene_foundation::spine::values::rect::Rect<u32>
pub type skene_terminal::Size = skene_foundation::spine::values::size::Size<u32>
pub type skene_terminal::Spacing = skene_foundation::spine::values::flex::Spacing<u32>
pub type skene_terminal::Token<T> = skene_foundation::spine::token::Token<T, u32>
pub type skene_terminal::TokenValue = <skene_terminal::ActivePlatform as skene_foundation::spine::platform::Platform>::TokenValue
pub type skene_terminal::Value<T> = skene_foundation::spine::leaf::Value<T, skene_terminal::Condition>
